With the introduction of Auto Recovery, the platform now proactively detects and repairs gaps in missing or incomplete recordings without requiring any user intervention, running on predefined schedules during off-peak windows to ensure optimal system performance. 
In addition to automated processes, customers will gain access to Manual (On-Demand) Recovery, empowering authorized users to initiate recovery requests directly from the User Interface. 
This capability provides two flexible modes: 
  * Immediate Recovery: Ideal for addressing urgent, short-duration gaps (up to 1 hour) as soon as resources become available. 
  * Off-Peak Recovery: Designed for larger gaps (up to 12 hours), allowing users to schedule recovery during configured off-peak windows to minimize impact on live operations


### In-app messaging support for mobile apps
Webex Contact Center will support in-app messaging alongside web chat for end-customer messaging support over mobile apps. Businesses can embed Webex Connect's mobile Android and iOS SDKs into their app and leverage Connect flows to offer self-service, orchestrate automation, and route to live agents. The SDK will include chat capabilities such as real-time messaging, rich interactive messages, typing indicators, read receipts, quick replies, and conversation history. Administrators can configure mobile messaging support from chat assets, while agents, supervisors, analysts, and developers use their current interfaces such as Desktop, Analyzer, etc. to handle and monitor these interactions.
Expected General Availability date: 20th August, 2026.
### Explore the Reimagined Homepage in Analyzer
Analyzer is launching a refreshed home page experience in Webex Contact Center. The new home page brings visualizations, dashboards, and folders into one organized starting point, so users can find, view, and manage reporting content more easily.
Users can search across reporting content, access recent and favorite items, apply filters, and use common actions such as View, Edit, Duplicate, Move, Schedule, Export, and Delete based on their permissions.
Existing reports, dashboards, permissions, report creation, and output behavior remain unchanged. Some actions may continue to open existing Analyzer flows while the broader Analyzer refresh experience is delivered incrementally.
The old Analyzer skin is being retired. For more information, see announcements.
**Expected General Availability date: 20 Aug, 2026**
### AI Agent transcripts for voice interactions
Webex Contact Center will soon make AI Agent transcripts for voice interactions available after a call through a unified transcript storage experience. Post-call transcripts will be stored securely and made available to authorized users and applications through the Search and Captures APIs, helping administrators and downstream systems access AI Agent and human-agent transcript data from a consistent location. This update will also add transcript availability indicators in the Search API response, making it easier to identify whether AI Agent and human-agent transcripts are available for an interaction.
As part of this update, the transcript format is changing. Customers and partners who consume human agent-customer transcripts today may need to update their integrations or downstream solutions to support the new format.
### Share Your Experience with In-App Satisfaction Ratings and Feedback
We’re excited to announce a new way for agents and supervisors to share feedback directly from the Webex Contact Center desktop. Soon, you’ll be able to rate your experience and provide valuable comments using a convenient in-app feedback tool.
Look for the new feedback icon above the Help button on your desktop’s navigation bar. Clicking the icon will open a quick feedback form, where you can select a rating (1 to 5 stars) and add written comments—helping us improve your product experience. Your feedback is saved if you close the form and return later, and you’ll receive a confirmation once your input is submitted.
### Enhancements on encryption of file attachments in Webex Contact Center
We are pleased to announce data security enhancements for Webex Contact Center customers using digital channels to interact with their clients. Webex Contact Center will soon support the encryption of file attachments handled via digital channels while providing each organization with a unique encryption key. This enhanced encryption feature empowers customers to retain complete control over their encryption keys, ensuring jurisdiction over their data.
All file attachments in inbound or outbound communications will be secured using key-based end-to-end encryption. Each tenant is granted access to a unique key generated by the Webex Key Management System (Webex KMS). The KMS facilitates real-time encryption and decryption of attachments within the solution and promotes a seamless conversation flow.
Cisco will also provide a Software Development Kit (SDK) that companies can use to decrypt these file attachments before forwarding them to other business applications such as CRM, ERP, Billing, or WFO. Cisco will make this SDK available for public access in the first quarter of 2025, along with a development guide and code samples, to assist customers in updating the integration with those applications.
This encryption enhancement will be enabled for all Webex Contact Center customers a few months after the public SDK becomes available, giving customers enough time to prepare. All new Webex Contact Center tenants will have this enhancement enabled by default once the feature becomes available.
We will share specific dates in upcoming articles and other communication channels. Stay tuned!
### Answer calls on your device directly from Agent Desktop
We're introducing a new enhancement for agents using Webex Calling devices or phones. If you're using a Webex Calling device that supports remote call answering, you can answer calls directly from the Agent Desktop! Previously, agents were notified of the call but had to answer it using their device. Now, there is no more switching between screens or devices-just click to answer. 
To avail of this feature Agents need to [Toggle Manage Webex Calling Calls in Agent Desktop to ON](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/hdoryv/Set-up-and-manage-your-notifications#enable-or-disable-notification-settings). 
  * **If you have one Webex Calling device:**
    * You can answer calls directly from the Agent Desktop—no additional setup needed.
  * **If you have multiple Webex Calling devices:**
    * Go to your **Profile Settings** and select your preferred device for answering calls. Note that the Webex App is a Webex calling device.


![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/490001-500000/492001-493000/492212.png)
For more information, see [Optimize Webex Contact Center Desktop call notifications](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/u5dbtfb/) and [Set up and manage your notifications](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/hdoryv/).
### Support for Agent variables in Quick replies/Templates
Administrators can now easily populate agent name variables when configuring Quick Replies / Templates for their agents. As part of this enhancement, Admins can leverage the following agent variables: first name, last name, and full name within the Quick Replies. This enhancement enables use cases to add the agent's signature when responding via quick replies, ultimately improving agent productivity.
### Remove the dependency of Contact WrapUp Events
We will enhance the CAR records to include a new Activity State for WrapUp complete. The string for new activity state will be 'wrapup-completed'. This does not impact any existing calculation or reports.
### Callback strategy for Campaign callbacks
Callback strategy of Native campaign manager will give administrators explicit control over how customer-requested campaign callbacks are handled when the callback attempt doesn't successfully connect the customer to an agent.
With this enhancement, administrators and supervisors will be able to configure callback-specific behavior directly in the campaign canvas instead of relying only on standard contact attempt settings. 
Administrators will be able to 
  * Define a dedicated Telephony outcome set for callback
  * Control how callback retries should behave
  * Configure Additional time for callback retries beyond the agreed callback window


This helps Native campaign manager manage callback follow-up more precisely while keeping callback attempts aligned to campaign scheduling rules.
### Webex Contact Center for ServiceNow Integration—Release 1
The new Webex Contact Center for ServiceNow delivers out-of-the-box voice integration, embedding Webex Contact Center telephony and routing directly into ServiceNow’s native Contact Center Integration Framework (CCIF) agent experience. Organizations can use Webex Contact Center as the voice and routing engine while ServiceNow serves as the single pane of glass for handling customer interactions. This integration introduces native call controls, reduces context switching, improves agent efficiency with real-time transcription, and surfaces mid-call and post-call voice summaries directly on the ServiceNow record. It also enables ServiceNow Now Assist to leverage conversation context for richer case assistance and follow-up workflows. Release 1 focuses on voice integration and sets the foundation for future omnichannel expansion.
Key Capabilities 
  1. Native Integration with ServiceNow—Integrates Webex Contact Center telephony, voice, and routing directly into ServiceNow’s CCIF-based agent workspace for a seamless unified experience. 
  2. Enhanced Agent Experience —Provides native call controls in ServiceNow, eliminating context switching and enabling agents to manage calls directly from the record. 
  3. Webex Contact Center-Powered Voice Routing—Enables organizations to use existing Webex Contact Center telephony, routing, WebRTC, and recording capabilities while ServiceNow remains the single agent interface. 
  4. Real-Time Productivity powered by Webex AI—Delivers live transcription plus mid-call and post-call voice summaries directly on the ServiceNow record. 
  5. Now Assist Enabled—Makes conversation context available in ServiceNow to support Now Assist AI-driven workflows, case assistance, and follow-up actions.


### Inline images in email templates and transcripts
Admins can use inline images in email templates and preview inline images in digital interaction transcripts. This helps teams preserve the full context of customer email conversations, including images that appear in messages and historical replies, while continuing to support security controls such as malware scanning and encryption within Cisco boundaries.
### AI-Powered prompt assistance for autonomous AI agents
We are excited to announce an upcoming upgrade to Webex AI Agent Studio designed to simplify and enhance your agent development workflow. We are introducing AI-optimized prompt creation using which you can easily generate a prompt from a simple description, optimize existing instructions using prompt-writing best practices, and review AI-generated changes before accepting them. A new dedicated Instructions tab provides the above capabilities.
These updates help you create clearer, more consistent agent instructions with less trial and error.
### Full Customer Journey View in Analyzer
We are excited to introduce the new Show Full Customer Journey toggle in the Contact Center Overview Historical dashboard helps you view contact metrics across the full journey of an interaction, including movement across queues, agents, consults, transfers, and conferences. Use this view to better understand how end-to-end view of the interaction are handled, while still switching back to the default queue view when needed.
**Expected General Availability date: 20 Aug, 2026**
## September 2026
### Bring your own messaging channel
We'll be launching Bring Your Own messaging Channel (BYOC) in Webex Contact Center, a new capability that lets businesses integrate externally managed custom messaging channels that are not natively supported by Webex Contact Center. With BYOC, developers can inject and update custom channel interactions through APIs, orchestrate messaging flows and routing, relay outbound messages through webhooks, and enable agents and supervisors to handle, monitor, and report on these conversations alongside other channels in a unified contact center experience. 
**Expected General Availability date: 17 Sep, 2026**
### Work Items in Webex Contact Center
Your businesses will soon have the power to manage non-conversational tasks, such as support tickets, backend system tasks, and other custom work items, directly within the Webex Contact Center workspace. By treating these tasks as a first-class channel type, you can route, prioritize, and monitor custom Work Items with the same ease and control as voice and native digital interactions.
**What's coming:**
• **Seamless Integration:** Inject Work Items via API and leverage a consistent omni-channel reporting view that includes all your critical business work items.
• **Unified Task Management:** Agents can handle Work Items alongside voice and native digital interactions in a single, streamlined desktop workspace.
• **Intelligent Routing:** Webex Contact Center Flow Designer is expanding to support Work Items alongside the voice flows currently available, enabling custom self-service and routing logic for your business tasks.
• **Enhanced Visibility:** Supervisors gain new real-time monitoring and reporting capabilities, ensuring complete visibility into agent utilization and task lifecycle progress.
**Expected General Availability date: 24 Sep, 2026**
### Whisper Coach in Supervisor Desktop
Whisper Coach will soon be available in Supervisor Desktop for eligible voice interactions. Administrators can control access by assigning the required permissions to supervisors, enabling authorized users to privately coach agents during active calls.
### Explore Customer Insight Reports in Analyzer
Analyzer is introducing a new Customer Insight Report that helps analysts and reporting users understand customer activity across interactions. The report uses Customer Entity data to bring related customer interactions into a customer-level view, with insights such as frequent intents, primary channel, contact activity, and Auto-CSAT. Teams can use the report to identify repeat contacts, monitor customer effort, spot journey patterns, and better understand where customers may need follow-up or support. Insights are available only for interactions captured from the Beta or GA enablement date onward; historical interactions aren’t included. 
**Expected General Availability date: 3 Sept, 2026**
### Auto-accept digital interactions
Administrators will be able to enable Auto accept interactions in a desktop profile so that assigned agents don’t have to manually accept ACD offered digital interactions. The setting applies to new interactions and interactions transferred to a queue or directly to an agent. It will be off by default, and existing desktop profiles will retain the off setting. The feature requires a Standard or Premium Agent license.
Expected General Availability date: 03 Sep, 2026
### Enhanced digital transcripts in vCon format
Webex Contact Center is enhancing digital transcripts with a standardized vCon format, richer interaction and participant metadata, and complete message payloads. Developers and internal Webex Contact Center services will be able to retrieve point-in-time transcripts for active interactions through a new API and post-interaction transcripts through the Captures API. These enhancements will help support AI capabilities such as summaries, quality management, topic analytics, and sentiment analysis.
**Expected General Availability date: 03 Sep, 2026**
### Configure Business Hours for queue metrics
Administrators will be able to map one Business Hours profile to voice and digital inbound queues. Webex Contact Center will use working shifts, holidays, and working or non-working overrides to calculate Business Hours wait-time and service-level metrics while keeping existing overall metrics available. A referenced Business Hours profile can’t be deleted until its queue mapping is removed.
Expected General Availability date: 03 Sep, 2026
## Planned Features
### Webex AI Quality Management: Enable evaluation and speech analytics for AI agent interactions
Administrators will be able to enable Evaluations and Speech Analytics for AI agent interactions and manage access to the AI Agent Performance Dashboard. With the appropriate permissions, supervisors will be able to assign AI agents to evaluation forms and review AI agents' performance alongside human agent performance.
A Webex AI Quality Management SKU is required to enable these capabilities.
### Webex AI Quality Management: Enable sentiment analysis for AI agent interactions
Administrators can enable sentiment analysis for AI agent interactions in Control Hub. When enabled, sentiment analysis will apply to all interactions in the organization where an AI agent is the first agent.
### Webex AI Quality Management: Configure granular access for Agent Central
We are introducing new, granular permission controls for Agent Central dashboard, giving administrators full control over the performance insights shared with agents. Admins can now precisely define access levels, determining which agents can view Agent Central page, and team leaderboard and team performance comparisons. Additionally, administrators can manage access to interaction-level drilldowns, including evaluations, recordings, and transcripts
A Webex AI Quality Management SKU is required to enable these capabilities.
. 
### Flow Designer MCP server for agentic flow management
Webex Contact Center will soon introduce Model Context Protocol (MCP) server support for Flow Designer, enabling agentic AI apps and automation tools to securely work with Contact Center flows through standardized tooling and interfaces.
This feature provides a governed foundation for agentic flow operations such as flow generation, troubleshooting, validation, and migration, while keeping administrators in control of published changes, organizational access and enabled actions. With this capability, authorized tools can help read, create, generate, update, and validate flows across the organization using agentic app integrations with Flow Designer. This unlocks various bulk administration use cases including bulk searching across flows, making controlled updates across flows including patches and edits.
Customers will onboard this capability through the Webex Agentic Platform. Administrators can enable the Flow Designer MCP server from Control Hub - Agentic Apps settings, with granular control over which MCP tools are enabled for the organization.
### Simplify Automation with Generic Action Fulfillment Flows
We are excited to announce that Generic Action fulfillment flows will soon be available to enhance your automation capabilities. This feature allows you to trigger flows automatically without an entry point.
Designed specifically for quick fulfillment tasks and back-office automation, these flows are ideal for data lookups, complex calculations, and payload transformations. You will be able to utilize a robust set of flow control, utility activities, functions and subflows.
Stay tuned for further updates as we prepare to roll out this new functionality to streamline your workflow logic and data management.
### Personalized AI Routing
We are excited to announce the upcoming launch of Personalized AI Routing for Webex Contact Center. This feature moves beyond rigid, manual routing rules by automatically adapting to real-time contact patterns and workforce conditions, using customer intent and agent insights to make intelligent, dynamic decisions.
**Why It Matters for Your Business**
Personalized AI Routing drives measurable impact by intelligently matching customers to the best-fit agents. Our AI-driven approach delivers:
  * **Reduced Handle Time:** Minimize effort and operational costs by matching customers to the right agent instantly for quicker resolutions.
  * **Increased Customer Satisfaction (CSAT):** Deliver a seamless, personalized experience that builds long-term brand loyalty.
  * **Custom KPI Optimization:** Whether your goal is to boost upsell success or improve conversion rates or any other custom KPI, you can now optimize the outcomes by AI routing for any Business metrics.


Stay tuned for more updates as we prepare to roll out this powerful enhancement to your Webex Contact Center experience.
### AI Assistant for Flow Designer
We're excited to announce the upcoming release of AI Assistant in Webex CX Flow Designer - an intelligent copilot experience that brings in contextual help directly into your workflow during the design process. 
This includes:
  * **Contextual Inline Help** : Get instant, relevant guidance based on your actions or selected components-right where you need it.
  * **Visual Learning Resources** : Access embedded help articles, links, and how-to videos to quickly learn best practices.
  * **Intelligent Search** : Find answers fast with an intuitive search powered by the AI Assistant. Includes support for flow documentation, JSON parsing, Python and JavaScript functions, and more.
  * **User History** : Threads to keep a track of recurring queries.


Why it matters
Traditional workflows require users to switch between the Flow Designer and external documentation, slowing productivity and increasing frustration. The AI Assistant eliminates these pain points by keeping you within the application, reducing time-to-value, and accelerating flow creation with just-in-time help.
**Who Benefits?**
  * **Administrators** : Streamline flow creation and reduce the learning curve.
  * **Developers** : Build, customize, and optimize flows faster with easy to access knowledge.


### AI Wait Time in Webex Contact Center
You can now enhance your contact center’s customer experience with AI wait time, which uses machine learning to provide better estimated wait times. This feature will capture key data points enabling advanced predictive models tailored to customer organizations.
Administrators will have flexible controls, including an organization-level toggle and queue-specific enablement options, to seamlessly manage this capability. AI wait time will be integrated under a unified **AI Features** tab, consolidating Cisco’s Customer Experience AI offerings for streamlined administration and improved operational insights.
## **Supervisor desktop**
  * The Team Performance Details page operates at optimum performance for up to 500 agents. A supervisor with access to more than 500 agents might not experience the best performance with the Team Performance Details page. 
  * When agent 2 is conferencing into a call that is being handled by agent 1, agent 2’s details does not show all the call metadata. Also, when agent 2 is consulting with agent 1, the call details are not available on agent 2’s active interaction details modal. These issues will be fixed in a future release as an enhancement. 
  * The default layout Cisco provides to the Global Layout section doesn’t support cross launching the Webex app. As an alternative, you can customize any of the layouts to add the cross-launch option.
  * When conferencing with a dial number mapped to an entry point wherein 2 agents - agent1 and agent2 are conferencing, the second agent’s contact status displays “Connected” instead of “Conferencing” in the Team Performance Details page. Note that consulting and conferencing a dial number mapped to an entry point is still in limited availability. 
  * There are differences in data between Supervisor Desktop reports and Homepage. For more information, see [Comparison of KPI Cards in Supervisor Desktop and Analyzer](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nqpc4zf/Comparison-of-KPI-Cards-in-Supervisor-Desktop-and-Analyzer).
  * Supervisors must have unique number assignments. Supervisor1 signs into the Supervisor Desktop with a number. Supervisor1 needs to sign out and sign in with a different number, so that Supervisor2 can sign into Supervisor Desktop with the same number used by Supervisor1. If you use the same phone number for different supervisors, contact your account team, partner, or Cisco support for assistance.
  * To save the Inactivity Timeout setting on Control Hub, you must enter a value for the Auto Wrapup interval setting. However, this isn't applicable when you use the Management Portal for tenant level settings.


## **Courtesy callback routing to capacity-based teams is not supported**
Courtesy callback isn't supported with capacity-based teams (CBT). CBTs have no individual agents assigned to them, and courtesy callback requires an Agent ID to function. Therefore, if courtesy callback flows to an entry point or a queue served by a CBT, the call fails.
## July 31, 2026
## Retirement of the Old Analyzer Skin
The old Analyzer skin is being retired. As of **August 20, 2026** , users will no longer be able to access the old Analyzer skin. However, users can continue to access and switch between the New User Experience and the Old User Experience after the retirement date.

## July 30, 2026
## Management Portal Migration and Decommissioning Status
The Management Portal has been decommissioned on July 30, at 9:00 AM UTC and is no longer available. 
This change is part of our commitment to a unified and streamlined administrative experience. By migrating these features to Control Hub and the Desktop, we’re centralizing our features to provide a more efficient and cohesive workflow for all administrators. The table summarizes the migration status of Management Portal features. The migration completes the full decommissioning of the portal. 
**Management Portal decommissioning status**  
|  **Entity**  |  **Status on Management Portal**  |  **Availability status on Control Hub/Desktop**  |  **Link to Coming Soon/What's New**  |  
| --- | --- | --- | --- |  
|  **Routing Strategies**  | Decommissioned   | Available on Control Hub  |  [What's New](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nv7abhz/What's-new-for-administrators-in-Webex-Contact-Center#section_hl1_pp2_s3c)  |  
|  **Audit Trails**  | Decommissioned  | Available on Control Hub  |  [What's New](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nv7abhz/What's-new-for-administrators-in-Webex-Contact-Center#section_svn_fqf_c3c)  |  
|  **Call Recording Schedules**  | Decommissioned  | Available on Control Hub  | NA   |  
|  **Recording Management**  | Decommissioned  | Available on Control Hub  |  [What's New](https://help.webex.com/en-us/nv7abhz/What's-new-for-administrators-in-Webex-Contact-Center#section_syd_qtp_v3c)  |  
|  **Threshold Alerts**  | Decommissioned  | Available as Alert Rules on Desktop > Configurations.*  | NA  |  
|  **Call Monitoring**  | Decommissioned  | Available as Schedule listening on Desktop > Team performance.**  |  [What's New](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/o6f77s/What's-new-for-supervisors-in-Webex-Contact-Center#section_ob1_rrt_bkc)  |  
|  Whisper Coaching  | Decommissioned  | To be available from 24th September as Whisper Coach on  Desktop > Interactions > Active interaction > Whisper coach.  |  [Coming Soon](https://help.webex.com/en-us/nv7abhz/What%27s-new-for-administrators-in-Webex-Contact-Center#topic_D8159C942D4EE10D3C86F7C043C86722).  |  
|  **URLs for BRE/Analyzer/Desktop/Webex WFO/Campaign Manager**  | Removed  | Available on Control Hub  |  [What's New](https://help.webex.com/en-us/nv7abhz/What's-new-for-administrators-in-Webex-Contact-Center#concept-template_589409be-a697-412d-b211-86a7ded7262a)  |  
|  **Full decommissioning of** **Management Portal** ***  | The Management Portal has been fully decommissioned on July 30, 2026, and is no longer available.  |  
*Existing Threshold Alert rules are migrated to Alert Rules in Desktop because of higher adoption. Organizations with a Threshold Alert configured on July 30 can use Alert Rules in Desktop; all others receive access when the feature becomes generally available. External administrators and users with unlicensed administrator profiles no longer have access to Alert Rules. Licensed administrators and supervisors retain access when they are assigned the required permission.
** Existing Call Monitoring schedules haven't been migrated to Schedule listening yet because of low adoption. Administrators must create new schedules in Desktop.
***An updated version of this guide is now available. Please begin using the [Set up and Administration Guide](https://help.webex.com/en-us/collection/cl-np50asw) immediately and update your bookmarks with the new link.
See [decom schedule article](https://help.webex.com/en-us/hpdsqx) to view the new location of features that have moved to Desktop.
## May 20, 2026
## Webex Contact Center for ServiceNow CTI connector qualified for ServiceNow Australia release
Webex Contact Center has completed qualification for the ServiceNow Australia family release for the Webex Contact Center for ServiceNow CTI connector.
The ServiceNow Store listing now shows Australia compatibility for the [Cisco Webex Contact Center CTI Connector](https://store.servicenow.com/store/app/3fe073a61ba46a50a85b16db234bcb44) for ServiceNow, enabling customers upgrading to ServiceNow Australia to continue using Webex Contact Center voice workflows inside ServiceNow.
Australia is an important ServiceNow release because it reinforces two paths for Webex Contact Center and ServiceNow customers: continued support for the current CTI OpenFrame-based connector, and the forward-looking CCIF-based integration path. CTI connector provides the established embedded connector experience today, while CCIF brings next-gen Webex Contact Center voice and routing deeper into ServiceNow’s native contact center framework for a more unified agent workspace experience.
This qualification covers the current CTI connector while also validating the CCIF-based integration in Beta, where Webex Contact Center is the primary voice and routing engine, and ServiceNow remains the primary agent workspace with native call controls and deeper workflow context.
## Decommissioning of Dial Plans
Dial plan configuration is being removed from desktop profiles and Control Hub. As of **May 25, 2026** , new customers no longer see dial plan configuration in desktop profiles or as a separate Control Hub configuration. Starting **June 25, 2026** , existing customers will also no longer see or manage dial plan configuration, and the related API will be decommissioned.
This change removes an unsupported configuration experience to reduce confusion and improve the Control Hub administration experience.
## March 23, 2026
## Removal of Contact History Widget from Default Agent Desktop Layout
The Contact History widget will be deprecated from the Default Agent Desktop Layout effective May 15, 2026. The Contact History widget was originally developed to provide agents with a view of past interactions.
Since the Contact History widget provides limited capabilities, we have decided to invest in the Customer Journey widget, which is more comprehensive in terms of the features supported and provides a progressive profile and full omnichannel interaction history for deeper context. Please note that this change only affects the default layout; customers using custom desktop layouts will continue to be able to use the Contact History widget without impact.
## March 11, 2026
## Webex WFO: Manage Workflow Control Sets on the Web
Webex WFO is moving Workflow Control Set management from the WFM client to the web for a more streamlined, browser-based administration experience. For more information refer to [Manage workflow control sets and profiles in the web](https://wfohelp.com/doc/Content/user-guides/wfm-settings/manage-workflow-control-sets-web.htm).
This functionality will transition to the web-based platform through a phased rollout between March 31, 2026, to April 6, 2026. Effective April 6, 2026, all WFM workflow control set operations will be exclusively managed via the web portal. To maintain uninterrupted service, ensure that all WFM workflow control sets are fully migrated by this date.
This change supports simpler access, centralized control, and ongoing platform modernization.
## November 19, 2025
## Analyzer Home Page: Temporary Unavailability for Performance Improvements
We're continuously working to enhance your experience with Analyzer. As part of ongoing improvements, the Analyzer Home page will be temporarily unavailable while we implement a new design and performance upgrades. During this period, the rest of the application will remain fully functional. We appreciate your understanding and look forward to bringing you a faster, more seamless Analyzer experience soon. Stay tuned for future updates.
## August 25, 2025
## Webex Contact Center Analyzer: Stock Report Cleanup and Revamp
We are initiating a cleanup and revamp of Stock Reports in Webex Contact Center Analyzer. In the initial phase, support for 39 lower-impact, less frequently used stock reports will be discontinued by January 7, 2026. This effort reflects our commitment to providing more focused, purposeful, and business-driven reports by leveraging the advanced capabilities of Analyzer.
Additionally, we will prioritize delivering reports that offer clear business insights by developing new reports, improving existing ones, and retiring those that no longer fulfill their intended purpose or provide substantial value. Please be assured that the visualizations within dashboards will remain unaffected and will continue to function as usual.

## August 19, 2025
## End of Support for Analyzer Beta from Webex Contact Center
Webex Contact Center ended support for the Analyzer Beta on August 19, 2025. The Analyzer Beta was originally developed to enhance the application's usability. 
Since the Analyzer Beta supported limited capabilities, we have decided to invest in simplifying and improving the usability of the current Analyzer, which is more comprehensive in terms of the features supported and is the primary application used by most of the users. 
## July 14, 2025
## Webex Contact Center Incident and Maintenance Notifications Moving to the Global Service Status Page
Starting August 1, 2025, Webex Contact Center will consolidate its incident and maintenance notifications under the [Global Service Status](https://status.webex.com/) page. After this date, notifications for incidents, maintenance windows, and occasional complex feature rollouts will no longer be sent via CJPIM email or Webex room notifications.
To continue receiving updates on the operational status of Webex services and the scheduled maintenance calendar, please subscribe to email or RSS feed notifications on the [Global Service Status](https://status.webex.com/) page. For updates on feature releases, you can subscribe to the [What’s New](https://help.webex.com/en-us/nv7abhz) and [Coming Soon](https://help.webex.com/en-us/nv7abhz/What's-new-for-administrators-in-Webex-Contact-Center#topic_D8159C942D4EE10D3C86F7C043C86722) help pages.
Please visit these pages and subscribe to ensure you continue to receive important notifications related to Webex Contact Center. For more information on how to subscribe to email/rss feeds, visit [Subscribe to Webex Contact Center Incident and Maintenance Alerts](https://help.webex.com/en-us/obit3d) article.
## June 26, 2025
## Data Explorer Shutdown in Webex WFO
The shutdown timeline for Webex WFO’s Data Explorer has been extended from June 30, 2025 to July 30, 2025. After July 30, 2025, customers will no longer have access to Data Explorer and are expected to leverage Webex WFO Insights for all reporting needs. With the recent launch of Classic WFM datasets, this extension provides Classic WFM customers with additional time to familiarize themselves with Insights and move comfortably to the new reporting experience. 
Here is a summary of Webex WFO Insights:
Insights is a modern, fully-featured BI solution with a range of features and improvements designed to significantly enhance your data access and visibility within Webex WFO.
**Reasons to get excited about Insights** :
  * The Insights experience is designed for streamlined data exploration and analysis, while being easy for non-technical users to independently create reports and dashboards.
  * Highly customizable to help accelerate decision making
  * Offers a broad range of visualizations
  * Suitable for both efficient ad-hoc analysis and rich dash-boarding.


Here is a short video to provide an overview of the capabilities that Insights brings to the table: <https://wfohelp.com/doc/Content/user-guides/insights-bi/get-started/how-insights-works.htm?tocpath=Data%20Analysts%7cInsights%7c_____1>
If your organization has already completed the transition to Insights, you also have the option to manually disable Data Explorer before it is automatically decommissioned. For more information, see <https://wfohelp.com/doc/Content/user-guides/insights-bi/turn-off-data-explorer.htm>
## April 2, 2025
## Migration of Webex WFO Guides from Cisco Product Support page to Webex Help Center
The Webex WFO guides previously available on the [Cisco Product Support](https://www.cisco.com/c/en/us/support/contact-center/webex-workforce-optimization/series.html) page are now accessible directly from the [Webex Help Center](https://help.webex.com/en-us/) . When you click on these guides on the Cisco Product Support page, you will be redirected to their respective pages in the Webex Help Center.
**How to access the Webex WFO documentation from[Webex Help Center](https://help.webex.com/en-us/)**:
  * Navigate to Help by product > Customer Experience > Workforce Optimization
  * Choose **Webex Contact Center** and then click on the appropriate link to access the document that you need.


**How to access the Webex WFO documentation from Contact Center product page on[Webex Help Center](https://help.webex.com/en-us/products)**:
  * Navigate to Customer Experience > Contact Center > Webex Contact Center and choose the preferred persona.
  * Choose **Webex Workforce Optimization (WFO)** and then click on the appropriate link to open the document that you need.


## February 28, 2025
## Removal of Dashboard and configurations from Webex Contact Center’s Tenant Management portal
As communicated with effect from **April 1, 2025** Tenant Management Portal of Webex Contact Center is now no longer supporting Dashboards and the following configurations:
  * Entry Points
  * Queues
  * Site
  * Team
  * Users
  * Work Types
  * Auxiliary Codes
  * Desktop Profile
  * Address Book
  * Outdial ANI
  * Dial Plan
  * Global Variables
  * Multimedia Profiles
  * Desktop Layout
  * Skills
  * Entry Point Mappings
  * Reports


As a result, we aren’t updating the Setup and Administrator's Guide for these entities. For the most updated and relevant content, refer to the [Webex Contact Center Administrator's help](https://help.webex.com/en-us/ld-93x1hg-ContactCenter/Administrator?type=complex&subTab=Webex-Contact-Center) on Webex Help Center.
However, the following configurations are still accessible from the Tenant Management portal: 
  * Settings (accessible by selecting the name of your tenant under Provisioning)
  * Threshold Rules
  * User Profiles


All other configurations not included under Provisioning, and not explicitly mentioned in this update will remain available in the Tenant Management portal until further notice.
## September 25, 2024
## End of support for VPOP provisioning for new Webex Contact Center tenants 
The Webex Calling PSTN services add-in for Webex Contact Center was introduced earlier this year. This add-in allows Webex Contact Center customers to selfprovision SIP trunk connections directly in Webex Control Hub using Local Gateway functionality.
The Local Gateway replaces functionality previously provided by VPOP for new customers. The add-in also provides Contact Center customers access to Cisco Cloud connected PSTN providers.
With the general availability of the Webex Calling PSTN services add-in, Cisco will be removing the ability to provision new VPOP based contact centers. For all new Webex Contact Center orders Control Hub can be used to provision telephony integration.
This change won’t affect existing VPOP customers who can continue to use VPOP integrations currently. Cisco will communicate directly with VPOP customers in the future as Cisco transitions VPOP services.For more information on Webex Contact Center telephony integration, see [Set up voice channels for Webex Contact Center](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/2dputx/Set-up-voice-channels-for-Webex-Contact-Center).
## September 4, 2024
## End of Service announcement for Webex Contact Center Legacy Media Platform
Webex Contact Center introduced its next generation voice media platform over two years ago. The Real Time Media Service (RTMS) has delivered many new capabilities to our customers, including regional media support, WebRTC based agents and caller background noise removal. 
Over the last year, Cisco has been transitioning customer onto this new platform. This process is now almost complete and therefore we are announcing the retirement of our legacy voice media platform. The last day of operation for the legacy media platform will be 30th November 2024. All customers must have completed their transition to Real Time Media Service (RTMS) before this date.
Customers can check their operational media platform in Webex Control Hub. Selecting “Contact Center” and then “General” from Tenant Settings will show the current voice media platform. Current voice media platform should read “Real Time Media Service”. If this field contains any other value, please contact your partner or customer success manager with urgency to facilitate your transition to RTMS.
## July 25, 2024
## Exciting Update: Webex Contact Center Licensing Consumption and Reporting
We are pleased to announce an important update on [Webex Contact Center Licensing Consumption and Reporting](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/n9vhuwe/Webex-Contact-Center-license-consumption-and-reporting).
This updated article now includes enhanced sections on **Viewing Usage** and **How is Usage Determined?**. Additionally, we've introduced a new **Frequently Asked Questions** section to address common inquiries. This section covers topics such as the capabilities and limitations of surge protection, and clarifies that while it is currently not possible to entirely prevent overage charges, efforts are underway to develop a solution.
As mentioned in the article, these updates will be available across all Webex Contact Center data center regions by August 2024.
Thank you for your continued support as we strive to provide you with the most comprehensive and transparent service possible.
## July 17, 2024
## Discontinuation of Routing Strategies for New Tenants
Webex Contact Center will be discontinuing support of Routing Strategies for any new tenant onboarded on or after July 17, 2024. We recommend new tenants use Business Hours as an alternative. Existing customers may continue to modify their routing strategies; however, we strongly recommend managing schedules through Business Hours for existing customers as well. _We will announce the dates for deprecating the Routing strategies soon and will give appropriate time for customers to transition to business hours. Until then, there will be no impact to the Routing Strategies functionality for existing customers_.
For more information on Business Hours, see [Set up business hours for Webex Contact Center](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/dqekw4).
## May 29, 2024
## Removal of 'Custom Attribute'
We are streamlining our recording management portal to enhance your experience. As part of this effort, we have phased out the custom attributes feature. You can take advantage of our tagging system. Tags offer a similar experience, enabling you to easily organize and identify your recordings. 
## February 22, 2024
## Introducing Persona-Based What's New: A Customized Approach to Product and Service Updates
We are excited to announce a significant enhancement in how we deliver updates about our products and services. To provide you with the most relevant and targeted information, we have transitioned from a consolidated "What's New" article to persona-based "What's New" articles specifically tailored for Administrators, Supervisors, and Agents. This new approach offers key benefits such as personalized content, streamlined updates, and enhanced clarity. You will receive updates that are directly pertinent to your persona. This means no more sifting through information that may not apply to you. 
## December 18, 2023
## Migration of Webex Contact Center 2.0 Guides from Cisco Product Support Page to Help Center 
The Webex Contact Center 2.0 Guides that were published on Cisco product support page are now available directly from the Help Center. 
So, from now on, when you click on these guides from the Cisco product support page, you'll be redirected to their respective articles in the Help Center. Here are the guides that have been moved:

## September 26, 2023
## End of support announcement for native chat and email channels
Webex Contact Center announces end of support for its native chat and email channels by Dec 31, 2023. We recommend that customers who have currently deployed these channels for their business to upgrade to new digital channels. These new digital channels include Web chat, Email, SMS, Facebook Messenger, and WhatsApp, designed with smart and secure capabilities. Work with your Account Manager for licensing and deployment options. For more information about the new digital channels, see [Webex Contact Center New Digital Channels](https://www.cisco.com/c/en/us/td/docs/voice_ip_comm/cust_contact/contact_center/webexcc/SetupandAdministrationGuide_2/b_mp-release-2/wxcc-new-digital-channels.html).
